Moreover, no established benchmark jointly evaluates these two failure modes in the personalized multi-subject setting. We present MultiCompose, a composition framework that decouples per-concept personalization from multi-subject inference. A semantic preservation regularization maintains attribute binding capacity during fine-tuning, while a two-phase inference procedure automatically establishes subject layout and composes per-concept predictions through spatially exclusive masks. We further introduce MSP-Bench, a benchmark that jointly evaluates identity fidelity (ID), attribute binding accuracy (BIND), and attribute misalignment (MIS) through a dual-pathway protocol. Experiments show that MultiCompose outperforms existing methods on both conventional metrics and MSP-Bench, confirming the benchmark's ability to reveal failure modes that conventional metrics overlook. Code is available at https://github.com/I2-Multimedia-Lab/MultiCompose

</details>


### [102] [Towards Reliable and Reproducible Fetal Brain Biometry: A Deep Learning Approach Using MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03724)
*Francesca Maccarone,Marina Di Stefano,Giorgio Longari,Giulia Frigerio,Gloria Rizzato,Rocco Prudentino,Nivedita Agarwal,Tommaso Ciceri,Denis Peruzzo,Simone Melzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全自动框架，用于从3D超分辨率重建的胎儿脑MRI中获取可靠的脑部生物测量。该四步流程通过联合估计线性测量值和对应的解剖标志点，在150个公开数据集的脑部图像上取得了与现有方法相当或更优的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿脑部生物测量对定量评估脑发育、估算胎龄和检测异常至关重要。传统手工测量耗时且易变，现有自动化方法可重复性有限，特别是缺乏解剖学上可解释的标志点定位。因此需要一种既可靠又可解释的全自动方法。

Method: 提出四步流水线：1) 使用3D卷积神经网络从脑分割标签图中回归标志点坐标；2) 通过测量特定的几何优化来细化标志点位置；3) 计算生物测量参数；4) 在150个公开胎儿MRI数据集上进行评估，涵盖不同的扫描仪和协议，评估五种关键生物测量指标。

Result: 在150个体积（胎龄20-37周）上评估，与现有的唯一自动化流程相比，该方法在大多数测量中达到了相当或更高的准确性，同时提供了标志点定位的定量和视觉评估。

Conclusion: 该流水线为可靠的胎儿脑部生物测量提供了一种简单、高效、可解释且可扩展的解决方案，能够支持其集成到临床工作流程中。

Abstract: Fetal brain biometry is essential for quantitative assessment of brain development, supporting gestational age estimation, developmental monitoring, and detection of abnormalities. In clinical practice, measurements are manually performed, making them time-consuming and prone to variability. While automated approaches have been proposed, reproducible methods remain limited, particularly those providing anatomically interpretable landmark localization. We present a fully automated deep learning-based framework for reliable and reproducible brain biometry from 3D super-resolution-reconstructed fetal brain MRI. The proposed four-step pipeline derives biometric parameters by jointly estimating linear measurements and their corresponding anatomical landmarks. A 3D convolutional neural network is trained to regress landmark coordinates from brain segmentation label maps, followed by measurement-specific geometric optimization to refine landmark positions and compute measurements. The pipeline is evaluated on two publicly available fetal MRI datasets comprising 150 volumes (gestational age range: 20-37 weeks) acquired across different scanners and protocols, assessing five key biometric measurements across varying acquisition settings and providing a comprehensive evaluation of both measurement accuracy and landmark localization using quantitative metrics and visual assessment. Compared with the only available automated pipeline, the proposed method achieves comparable or improved accuracy for most measurements. In conclusion, we introduce a straightforward pipeline for reliable biometry estimations, with efficiency, interpretability and scalability that support integration into clinical workflows.

</details>


### [103] [TDVR: Joint Text Disambiguation and Viewpoint Reasoning for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03763)
*Qingxi Du,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Yining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDVR，一种无需训练的推理框架，通过构建语义3D场景图、利用LLM消除查询文本歧义、推理最优视角等方法，解决零样本3D视觉定位中查询模糊和视角缺失的问题，在ScanRefer数据集上超越现有最优方法15.25%（Acc@0.25）和14.46%（Acc@0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D视觉定位方法受限于模糊的查询文本和不足的视角信息，导致定位准确率不高。作者旨在解决这两个核心瓶颈，提升零样本场景下的3D视觉定位性能。

Method: TDVR框架包含四个关键步骤：(1)从3D点云检测实例构建语义3D场景图；(2)将原始查询、外观和空间关系描述输入LLM进行融合和消歧，通过链式思维推理生成结构化查询表示；(3)基于场景图和结构化查询进行视角推理，获得最优视角；(4)通过视角评分、混淆评分、类别评分和外观评分综合匹配目标对象，区分相似实例。

Result: 在公开ScanRefer数据集上，TDVR在Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5指标上分别超越现有最先进方法15.25%和14.46%，验证了其在视角推理、相似对象区分和模糊查询理解方面的优势。

Conclusion: TDVR通过训练-free的推理框架有效解决了零样本3D视觉定位中的查询文本歧义和视角缺失问题，显著提升了定位准确率，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D visual grounding aims to localize specific objects based on textual descriptions and 3D visual input. However, the effectiveness of existing methods is significantly hindered by the ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose TDVR, a training-free reasoning framework that disambiguates the input text and infers accurate viewpoints for zero-shot 3D visual grounding. First, we construct semantic 3D scene graph from the detected instances in the 3D point cloud. Subsequently, we put the original query, appearance and spatial relationship descriptions into the LLM for fusion, thereby disambiguating the initial input. We leverage chain-of-thought reasoning to generate the structured representation of disambiguated query. Then taking the scene graph and structured query as input, we get the optimal view via viewpoint reasoning to solve the problem of missing viewpoints during grounding. Based on the obtained optimal viewpoint, we further discriminate the distracting objects, enabling the model with the ability to distinguish similar instances. After that, we match the category text and appearance images with the query by computing the similarity of feature vectors. Finally, the target object was identified by integrating the viewpoint score, confusion score, category score, and appearance score. Compared with previous methods, our TDVR has stronger capabilities in viewpoint reasoning, similar object discrimination, and ambiguous query understanding. Experimental results on the public ScanRefer dataset show that our method outperforms the existing state-of-the-art methods by 15.25% and 14.46% in Acc@0.25 and Acc@0.5 respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our TDVR in addressing ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints.

</details>


### [104] [AgenticVAU: Multi-Agent Explore-Verify Reasoning for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03779)
*Yuxiang Duan,Huining Li,Ao Li,Shuai Feng,Lanju Kong,Ning Liu,Jian Zhang,Xingdong Sheng,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticVAU，一个无需训练的基于多智能体的视频异常理解框架。它将视频异常理解建模为探索-验证流程，通过四个专业化智能体（视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察、最终决策）协同工作，并利用锚点注册表作为共享证据记忆来组织观察结果，在ECVA、UCF-Crime和MSAD基准上超越零样本推理和强化学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解方法要么依赖专门训练导致泛化能力受限，要么在证据覆盖上有所欠缺；单智能体方案虽然支持自适应观察，但将探索、观察和决策集成在统一推理过程中，缺乏角色专业化和结构化的证据协调机制。

Method: AgenticVAU采用无需训练的多智能体框架，将视频异常理解建模为探索-验证过程。框架包含四个专门智能体：视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察和最终决策。各智能体通过锚点注册表（共享证据记忆）进行通信，以绑定每个观察结果。该框架交替执行广泛的时间探索、密集的局部验证和跨区间比较，直到收集到足够证据。

Result: 在VAU-Bench的ECVA、UCF-Crime和MSAD子集上进行的大量实验表明，AgenticVAU在性能上优于零样本推理和基于强化学习的基线方法，验证了多智能体协作在视频异常理解中的价值。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作框架通过角色专业化和结构化证据协调，能够有效提升视频异常理解任务的性能，无需依赖专门的训练流程，为视频异常理解提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) focuses on comprehensively interpreting abnormal events in videos, requiring models to identify anomalous occurrences, discover their supporting evidence, and explain the underlying causes beyond simple anomaly detection. Existing VAU methods often rely on specialized training or limited observations, restricting generalization or evidence coverage. Although single-agent alternatives support adaptive video observation, they still integrate exploration, observation, and decision-making within a unified reasoning process, offering limited role specialization and structured evidence coordination. To address these limitations, we present AgenticVAU, a training-free multi-agent framework that casts VAU as an explore--verify process, where the system first discovers potential anomalies and then verifies them through targeted observations. To achieve this, four specialized agents are introduced to handle visual-rule construction, search planning, video observation, and final decision, respectively. These agents communicate through an anchor registry, a shared evidence memory that binds each observation. Guided by this agent framework, AgenticVAU interleaves broad temporal exploration, dense local verification, and cross-interval comparison until sufficient evidence is collected. We conduct extensive experiments on the ECVA, UCF-Crime, and MSAD subsets of VAU-Bench, the results show that AgenticVAU outperforms zero-shot inference and reinforcement learning-based baselines, demonstrating the value of multi-agent collaboration for video anomaly understanding.

</details>


### [105] [OmniPack: Unified Token Compression for Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03812)
*Wanshun Su,Yang Shi,Feihu Liu,Ziwen Yu,Yan Min,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Haotian Wang,Shixuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Peng Wu,Chengfu Huo,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPack是一个免训练的框架，通过LLM前的结构压缩与LLM内的语义精炼相结合，在极低token预算下实现全模态大语言模型的高效部署，同时保持最优的性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni-LLM处理长且高冗余的视听token序列时计算开销大，需要激进的token压缩。然而现有方法在低token预算下表现不佳：LLM前压缩可能丢失结构重要和全局分布的证据，而LLM内压缩未能充分利用查询条件下的视听协作。

Method: OmniPack是一个训练免框架，包含两个阶段的协调策略：(1) 在LLM之前，通过模态特定重要性、全局覆盖和相似性感知合并来移除结构冗余；(2) 在多模态交互后，通过文本引导和视听协作来整合多样化、任务相关的表征。

Result: 在五个基准测试和三个Omni-LLM骨干网络上，OmniPack在不同保留比例下始终取得最佳性能-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，OmniPack保留98.0%的原始性能同时将FLOPs降至16.7%；在仅保留6.8% FLOPs的情况下仍能保持92.9%的原始性能。

Conclusion: OmniPack通过将LLM前的结构压缩与LLM内的语义精炼有机结合，成功解决了低token预算下现有方法性能退化的问题，为全模态大语言模型的高效部署提供了有效的免训练解决方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on audio-visual understanding tasks, but processing long and highly redundant visual and audio token sequences incurs substantial computational overhead, demanding aggressive token compression for efficient deployment. Existing methods often degrade at low token budgets: pre-LLM compression may discard structurally important and globally distributed evidence, whereas inner-LLM compression often underexploits query-conditioned audio-visual collaboration. To address these limitations, we propose OmniPack, a training-free framework that coordinates structural compression before the LLM with task-relevant semantic refinement within the LLM. Before the LLM, OmniPack removes structural redundancy through modality-specific importance, global coverage, and similarity-aware merging. After sufficient multimodal interaction, it further consolidates diverse, task-relevant representations through textual guidance and audio-visual collaboration. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks with three Omni-LLM backbones demonstrate that OmniPack consistently achieves the best performance-efficiency trade-off across diverse retention ratios, outperforming all existing methods. Notably, on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniPack preserves 98.0% of the original performance while reducing FLOPs to 16.7%, and still retains 92.9% of the original performance with only 6.8% of the original FLOPs.

</details>


### [106] [UHP Detection: LVLMs have their Unique Hallucination Pattern in the Consistency Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03817)
*Amir Mohammad Ezzati,Kiyan Rezaee,Bardiya Kariminia,Mohamad Amin Yousefi,Asal Mohammadjafari Mamaqani,Behrad Samimi,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UHP Detection框架，利用双轴（扰动模态：图像vs文本；逻辑极性：陈述vs否定）构建四组互补一致性度量，训练轻量级分类器检测LVLM幻觉，在AMBER和PhD数据集上较最强黑盒基线提升最高+18.72% AUC-ROC和+20.07% AUC-PR，且跨数据集验证表明该模式可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒幻觉检测方法仅通过单一一致性指标估计不确定性，但幻觉在不同行为探针下表现多样，单一度量不足以刻画其底层行为特征，因此需要构建更全面的不确定性表征框架。

Method: 提出UHP Detection，一个全黑盒框架，将幻觉建模为结构化不确定性模式，由两个轴定义：扰动模态（图像vs文本）和逻辑极性（陈述vs其否定）。二者交叉产生四组互补一致性度量，从中提取组内和组间特征，训练轻量级分类器进行幻觉检测。

Result: 在AMBER和PhD基准上对三个LVLM进行综合实验，UHP Detection一致优于现有黑盒和白盒基线，较最强黑盒方法最高提升+18.72% AUC-ROC和+20.07% AUC-PR。消融实验表明各组一致性提供互补信息，跨数据集评估显示该模式可跨基准泛化。

Conclusion: 四组一致性度量组合形成的结构化幻觉模式能有效表征模型不确定性，且幻觉行为反映模型特定的一致性模式，该模式可跨数据集泛化，为黑盒LVLM幻觉检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large vision--language models (LVLMs) demonstrate strong multimodal reasoning capabilities but remain prone to hallucination, where model predictions are not grounded in visual evidence. Existing black-box hallucination detection methods estimate uncertainty through a single consistency metric, implicitly assuming that model uncertainty can be adequately characterized by a single measure. However, hallucinations exhibit diverse manifestations of uncertainty across different behavioral probes, making a single measure insufficient to characterize their underlying behavior. We propose \emph{Unique Hallucination Pattern (UHP) Detection}, a fully black-box framework that models hallucination as a structured uncertainty pattern defined by two axes: perturbation modality (image vs.\ text) and logical polarity (a statement vs.\ its negation). Their intersection produces four complementary consistency groups that capture distinct manifestations of model uncertainty, from which both within-group and between-group features are extracted to train a lightweight classifier. Through comprehensive experiments on AMBER and PhD across three LVLMs, UHP Detection consistently outperforms prior black-box and white-box baselines, with improvements of up to $+18.72\%$ AUC-ROC and $+20.07\%$ AUC-PR over the strongest black-box methods. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that each consistency group contributes complementary information and that their combination forms a structured hallucination pattern. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluation shows that this learned pattern generalizes across benchmarks, indicating that hallucination behavior reflects a model-specific consistency pattern. \textbf{Code is publicly available at} https://github.com/amirezzati/uhpdet.

</details>


### [107] [FlowForm: Synergizing Fluid Physics with Topological Consistency for Satellite Flood Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03822)
*Zhang Weihui,Wang Ruizhi,Xu Hongye,Wang Huiqiong,Sun Li,Song Mingli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowForm，一个集成了SWE启发式潜在正则化与结构感知条件的卫星洪水合成框架。通过Flood Descriptor Module（FDM）在扩散瓶颈处对浅水方程残差施加可微惩罚，并利用Terrain Anchor Adapter（TAA）在U-Net四个编码器尺度注入深度、语义和边缘特征。同时构建了大规模高分辨率洪水前后配对数据集FloodScape。实验表明FlowForm在视觉保真度、配对图像相似性和洪水区域一致性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的配对卫星影像对于构建稳健的洪水评估模型至关重要，但此类数据特别是面向洪水图像生成的数据十分稀缺。现有生成式数据增强方法往往产生不合理的洪水空间布局并扭曲场景结构，亟需更可靠的洪水图像合成方法。

Method: 提出FlowForm框架，包含两个关键组件：（1）Flood Descriptor Module（FDM），在扩散模型瓶颈处的辅助潜在场中对稳态浅水方程残差施加可微惩罚，确保洪水水文学物理合理性；（2）Terrain Anchor Adapter（TAA），在U-Net的四个编码器尺度注入深度、语义和边缘特征以提供结构感知引导。此外构建了FloodScape数据集，包含灾前灾后配对的卫星影像。

Result: 除标准图像生成指标外，还评估了洪水区域一致性、对地理上留出的洪水事件的零样本泛化能力及对各组件的敏感性。在所有对比中，FlowForm在视觉保真度、配对图像相似性和洪水区域一致性上均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: FlowForm通过物理约束与结构感知条件的结合，有效解决了卫星洪水图像生成中空间布局不合理和场景结构失真的问题，为洪水评估模型的训练提供了更高质量的数据增强手段。

Abstract: Developing robust flood assessment models requires high-quality paired satellite imagery, yet such data remain scarce for flood-specific image generation. Although generative models provide a promising means of data augmentation, existing methods often yield implausible spatial layouts of flooded regions and distort scene structures. We propose FlowForm, a framework for satellite flood synthesis that integrates SWE-inspired latent regularization with structure-aware conditioning. The Flood Descriptor Module (FDM) imposes differentiable penalties on residuals of the steady-state Shallow Water Equation in auxiliary latent fields at the diffusion bottleneck. The Terrain Anchor Adapter (TAA) injects depth, semantic, and edge features at four encoder scales of the U-Net. We further curate FloodScape, a large-scale, high-resolution dataset comprising paired satellite images acquired before and after disasters. In addition to standard image-generation metrics, we evaluate the consistency of flooded regions, zero-shot generalization to a geographically held-out flood event, and sensitivity to individual components. Across all reported comparisons, FlowForm achieves higher visual fidelity, greater similarity between paired images, and stronger consistency of flooded regions.

</details>


### [108] [Geo-Embed: Towards Unified Multimodal Embeddings for Urban Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03826)
*Jiapeng Li,Yong Li,Junjie Zhou,Fan Zhang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMEB大规模多模态地理空间嵌入基准（45个城市评估任务、132万训练样本、28.6万评估查询）和Geo-Embed统一嵌入模型，通过指令条件化的查询-目标匹配处理异构地理空间输入，相比最强基线实现15.3%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型和基准主要围绕通用图像-文本匹配设计和评估，缺乏对地理空间应用中空间关系、细粒度语义和时间变化等异构任务的支持，统一嵌入空间是否能支撑这些任务尚不明确。

Method: (1) 构建GeoMEB基准，覆盖检索、视觉问答、变化检测、分类和视觉定位5类共45个城市评估任务，配套132万训练样本和28.6万评估查询；(2) 提出Geo-Embed模型，基于共享视觉-语言骨干架构，通过指令条件化的查询-目标匹配适配异构地理空间输入（单图、多图、文本、区域和掩码）。

Result: Geo-Embed在GeoMEB上取得最强综合表现，相比最强基线实现15.3%的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了围绕显式查询-目标关系（语义、跨视角、区域级和时间对应）组织训练与评估的地理空间嵌入模型的有效性，为该方向发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Geospatial and urban applications increasingly require models to compare heterogeneous evidence across street-view imagery, remote-sensing observations, text descriptions, region proposals, and temporal change cues. However, existing multimodal embedding models and benchmarks are still largely designed and evaluated around general-purpose image-text matching, leaving unclear whether unified embedding space can support heterogeneous geospatial tasks involving spatial relationships, fine-grained semantics, and temporal changes. To address this gap, we make three key contributions. First, we introduce GeoMEB, a large-scale multimodal embedding benchmark that standardizes 45 urban evaluation tasks across retrieval, visual question answering, change detection, classification, and visual grounding, together with training collections comprising 1.32M examples and 286K evaluation queries. Second, we present Geo-Embed, a unified embedding model that adapts a shared vision-language backbone to instruction-conditioned query-target matching over heterogeneous geospatial inputs, including single images, multiple images, text, regions, and masks. On GeoMEB, Geo-Embed achieves the strongest overall performance among representative multimodal embedders, with a 15.3% relative improvement over the strongest baseline. These results motivate future geospatial embedders that organize training and evaluation around explicit query-target relations, including semantic, cross-view, region-level, and temporal correspondence.

</details>


### [109] [LiteMVS: Efficient Multi-View Stereo with Foundation Distillation and Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03851)
*Tianbao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Shuyu Wu,Fanxing Li,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteMVS提出了一种轻量级多视图深度估计模型，将平面扫描几何推理与单目语义/结构先验（来自分割模型和视觉基础模型）相结合，通过语义描述符增强代价体、MoE自适应几何聚合以及蒸馏几何先验，在静态场景3D重建和后续4D表示学习中均表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVS方法依赖几何对应关系，在无纹理或重复区域容易失效；而单目深度模型虽有图像级先验但缺乏多视图几何约束。此外，机器人/具身智能场景不仅需要静态重建，还需要为时空一致的4D表示学习提供几何基础，因此需要一种兼具结构感知和时序扩展潜力的高效深度估计方法。

Method: LiteMVS将轻量级分割模型和大规模视觉基础模型提取的单目高级知识高效注入多视图立体框架中。具体包括：1) 用语义描述符丰富代价体积；2) 采用Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）机制实现跨深度假设的自适应几何聚合；3) 从视觉基础模型中蒸馏几何先验以增强单目引导，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在ScanNetv2和7-Scenes数据集上的实验表明，LiteMVS实现了高质量的深度预测和3D重建，同时保持了有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: LiteMVS不仅提升了静态场景中的深度估计与3D重建质量，还为后续的时间建模和4D表示学习提供了更可靠、更具结构感知能力的几何基础，展现了在机器人、AR和具身智能应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time 3D perception is crucial for robotics, augmented reality, and embodied intelligence applications. Existing multi-view stereo (MVS) methods primarily rely on geometric correspondences, which often fail in textureless or repetitive regions, while monocular depth models leverage strong image-level priors but lack robust multi-view geometric constraints. More importantly, in robotics and embodied manipulation scenarios, high-quality 3D geometry is not only essential for static reconstruction, but also serves as a critical foundation for learning temporally consistent 4D representations. To obtain visual representations with stronger structural awareness and greater potential for spatiotemporal extension, we present LiteMVS, a lightweight multi-view depth estimation model that integrates plane-sweep geometric reasoning with strong monocular semantic and structural priors. The central idea of LiteMVS is to efficiently inject high-level monocular knowledge, obtained from lightweight segmentation models and large-scale vision foundation models, into a multi-view stereo framework. In particular, LiteMVS enriches the cost volume with semantic descriptors and employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) formulation to enable adaptive geometric aggregation across depth hypotheses. Moreover, geometric priors distilled from vision foundation models further strengthen monocular guidance without increasing inference cost. Through this design, LiteMVS not only improves depth estimation and 3D reconstruction quality in static scenes, but also provides a more reliable geometric foundation for subsequent temporal modeling and 4D representation learning. Experiments on ScanNetv2 and 7-Scenes demonstrate that LiteMVS achieves high-quality depth prediction and 3D reconstruction while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [110] [CPrefix: A Combinatorial Tensor Framework for Structured Discrete Color Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03863)
*Yvan Richard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CPrefix，一种用于离散多通道映射的组合可观测表示框架，基于计数张量和Pascal单纯形结构，实现映射的表示、重建与结构分析，并在ICC显示与打印profile上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散多通道映射通常通过采样值表示，虽能精确评估但难以揭示其底层结构。现有方法缺乏对映射组合组织的理解，需要一种能同时支持表示、重建和结构分析的统一框架。

Method: 基于多项计数可观测诱导的计数张量，其支撑形成离散Pascal单纯形，作为潜在组合表示进行映射重建。该框架将映射的组合组织与测量值分离，通过统一张量框架实现表示、重建和结构分析。

Result: 在ICC显示和打印机profile上进行验证，通过潜在重建和感知色域传输实现精确重建，表明颜色映射具有良好的可观测表示；重建残差揭示了映射与所提出表示的兼容性。

Conclusion: 框架独立于观测量的物理解释，适用于颜色成像、光谱测量及其他离散系统中的结构化多通道映射。

Abstract: Discrete multi-channel mappings are typically represented through sampled values, providing accurate evaluations but limited insight into their underlying structure. We introduce CPrefix, a combinatorial observable representation for discrete mappings, realized within a unified tensor framework that enables representation, reconstruction, and structural analysis.
  The framework is based on a counting tensor induced by multinomial counting observables. Its support forms a discrete Pascal simplex, not as a constraint on the observable space, but as a latent combinatorial representation from which mappings are reconstructed. This formulation separates the combinatorial organization of a mapping from its measured values, exposing the observable structure underlying the mapping.
  The framework is validated on ICC display and printer profiles through latent reconstruction and perceptual gamut transport. Accurate reconstruction demonstrates that color mappings admit faithful observable representations, while reconstruction residuals provide insight into the compatibility of the underlying mapping with the proposed representation.
  Although demonstrated on color transformations, the framework is independent of the physical interpretation of the observables, making it applicable to structured multi-channel mappings arising from color imaging, spectral measurements and other discrete systems.

</details>


### [111] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuRA框架，通过动态选择与融合不同秩的适配模块来解决视觉-语言模型在分布偏移下测试时自适应（TTA）中固定秩配置导致的优化瓶颈，实现了跨域泛化和跨数据集基准上的最先进精度，同时显著降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于低秩适配（LoRA）的测试时自适应方法依赖静态秩配置，而视觉输入天然具有变化的信息密度，固定秩会导致复杂场景欠拟合、简单场景过拟合的优化折中问题。

Method: MuRA框架包含两个核心组件：1) Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition（多秩正交分解）提供知识保持的初始化；2) Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating（统一组件融合与连续路由器更新）学习语义到秩的映射。同时基于token级视觉复杂度动态选择并融合不同容量的适配模块，并提供数学证明其必要性和梯度稳定性。

Result: 在广泛的域泛化和跨数据集基准上，MuRA达到最先进的精度，同时显著降低计算与内存开销，且在最深视觉层表现最优。

Conclusion: 动态秩自适应机制是解决测试时自适应中固定秩瓶颈的有效途径，MuRA通过理论证明与实验验证，证明了该方法的必要性、梯度稳定性以及实际有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [112] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个胸片X光视觉语言模型（VLM），通过将辅助判别监督（焦点损失分类头和复合损失定位头）与奖励对齐生成（DAPO）统一起来，同时结合原生工具调用能力进行确定性测量，缩小了放射科医生需求与生成模型能力之间的差距。在报告生成、VQA和空间定位等多项基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸X光片系统存在关键缺口：临床实用的系统需要可调阈值的分类、空间定位和解剖测量能力，但当前VLMs要么忽视这些问题，要么将其视为分离任务，导致生成模型无法满足放射科医生的实际需求。

Method: CARE-X采用三种互补策略：(1) 在生成骨干网络上增加焦点损失分类头和复合损失定位头，与语言建模目标共同训练；(2) 提出Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) 算法，利用任务特定的奖励信号优化报告生成、VQA和空间定位；(3) 将Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct与原生工具调用能力结合，调用确定性测量工具同时保留完整视觉访问权限。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准的大多数指标上达到最先进性能；ReXVQA VQA准确率达94.0%（比次优基线高6.0个百分点）；生成式空间解码接近专用检测头的性能；混合推理在五个依赖测量的诊断条件下比仅感知的基线平均F1提升43.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化预测与生成任务能够相互增强，结合确定性工具调用的混合推理范式可以有效弥补VLM在测量依赖型诊断中的不足，为临床级胸X光片AI系统提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [113] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NCGR方法，用于补偿相机外参扰动导致的BEV检测性能下降问题。NCGR通过预测2D修正偏移并配合相机级门控来修正投影误差，无需显式估计完整的6自由度外参校正。在nuScenes动态外参扰动实验中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，显著优于BEVFormer（28.00%）和CAPE（33.23%），同时在干净外参下保持与BEVFormer相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的BEV 3D检测通常假设准确且固定的相机外参，但在实际场景中外参扰动会使空间交叉注意力（SCA）中的BEV参考点投影到错误位置，导致查询从错误区域采样特征，从而严重降低检测性能。现有方法需要显式估计完整的6自由度外参修正，作者希望避免这一复杂过程。

Method: NCGR为每个query-camera对预测一个2D修正偏移，并通过相机级门控对该偏移进行调制，用以修正基础投影后再进行原生可变形采样。训练阶段，构造条件和门控所需的扰动衍生量通过调度插值逐步替换为从相机特征预测的辅助标量，从而支持无扰动元数据的盲推理。同时使用权重共享的clean-teacher/perturbed-student配对结构，通过两个分支间的BEV一致性目标监督修正模块。

Result: 在nuScenes上使用模拟动态和静态外参扰动进行评估。五相机动态压力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，对比BEVFormer的28.00%和CAPE的33.23%。在干净外参条件下，NCGR保持与BEVFormer相当的性能。

Conclusion: NCGR有效解决了外参扰动下的BEV检测退化问题，通过学习噪声条件的门控修正来补偿投影误差，在扰动场景下显著提升检测性能，同时不牺牲干净外参下的性能，且无需推断时显式估计外参修正。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [114] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniEvo-RS是一个面向遥感密集标注的通用提示分割框架，通过代表性样本驱动的原型演化机制，实现无需训练的性能提升，统一了文本和视觉多种提示任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示驱动视觉-语言模型在遥感新场景、未见类别和视觉混淆背景下性能严重退化；同时已有统一范式主要依赖图像内特定提示，缺乏灵活的任务路由以适配多意图的实际批处理标注工作流。

Method: 1) 构建统一多种指令的提示数据集，在一个架构中统一文本驱动和视觉驱动提示，建立动态任务路由机制；2) 引入代表性反馈驱动的免训练原型演化机制，通过对比人工标注与初始预测，将预测错误蒸馏为正负原型，增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声，在固定预算聚类记忆下工作。

Result: 在大多数设置下达到了最先进水平；在少量代表性样本上的最小交互即可实现对未见类别在批处理标注过程中渐进式、免训练的精度提升。

Conclusion: UniEvo-RS成功统一了多样化的提示任务，并通过原型演化机制使得模型在批处理标注过程中能够以极小的交互代价持续改善未见类别的分割精度。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [115] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向流式视频对象分割（VOS）的追踪器构建方法。传统的离线高精度追踪器（如DAM4SAM）在真实的30fps流式协议下性能崩塌，原因是其记忆管道过于缓慢且无法感知目标是否存在。作者将记忆机制重建为帧率运行，通过模型内优化替代外挂回退，并使用单一学习到的存在信号来统一控制记忆写入、回溯范围、输出抑制和重新检测。实验表明该方法在四个基准和五个基线上是最强的流式追踪器，在时钟约束下恢复了几乎所有离线精度，在最难的内容上甚至超越其源头离线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS追踪器（如DAM4SAM）在离线评测中表现优异，但在真实的30fps流式协议下，由于记忆管道速度不足和缺乏目标存在性感知，其性能严重退化。作者希望揭示这一失败根源并重建面向流式的追踪系统。

Method: 提出\method{}方法，核心包括：(1) 对追踪器的记忆管线进行重建，使记忆机制本身以帧率运行，通过模型内优化而非外挂回退实现；(2) 设计单一学习到的存在信号，统一控制四个关键决策：哪些内容进入记忆、追踪器回溯多远、何时抑制输出、何时重新检测；(3) 通过机制分析证明固定策略不可行——对目标真正消失有帮助的控制会对目标难以看见时造成伤害，因此必须逐帧决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和五个现代基线上，\method{}是最强的流式追踪器，在30fps时钟约束下恢复了离线模型近全部的精度，在最难的内容上甚至超过了其所基于的离线模型。

Conclusion: 流式VOS追踪的关键在于让记忆管道以帧率运行并通过学习到的存在信号进行逐帧自适应控制，而非依赖固定的策略或外挂的降级措施。这证明"离线最优"与"流式最优"之间存在本质差异，需要为流式场景重新设计架构。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [116] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoFrame是一个无需训练的轻量级框架，通过利用VLM自身的推理反馈（熵门控预算调度和注意力引导候选提议）实现查询自适应的视觉证据调度，在长视频理解中达到更好的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法要么依赖固定预算和候选池的静态一次性选择（缺乏自适应性），要么采用基于智能体的多轮交互调度（高昂的计算开销），难以在准确率和效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: EcoFrame提出两个核心机制：(1) 熵门控预算调度——利用VLM的输出不确定性来判断当前证据是否充分，若充分则提前停止，否则逐步扩大帧预算；(2) 注意力引导候选提议——将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息密集区域进行密集局部搜索，同时注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU上的实验表明，EcoFrame在多个VLM骨干上取得更好的准确率-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-VL上平均准确率64.4超过BOLT的63.5，较AKS和BOLT有1.85倍加速，较智能体方法A.I.R.最高可达13.5倍推理加速且精度相当。

Conclusion: EcoFrame证明了利用VLM推理反馈进行自适应的证据调度是可行的，无需额外训练即可实现高效的长视频理解，在精度和效率上都优于现有方法。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [117] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化仿射预处理（NAP）方法，针对低比特量化中紧凑网络性能严重退化的问题，将归一化仿射参数识别为支配量化鲁棒性的低维高杠杆子空间，实现定向子空间优化。在PTQ中仅微调仿射参数以提升量化友好性，在QAT中采用交替QAT-NAP训练范式解耦特征学习与数值校准，实验证明NAP能恢复严重坍缩的低比特量化并超越全参数QAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上性能严重退化，根源在于主流全参数耦合训练范式忽视了参数子空间的异质性。紧凑网络的特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差。传统PTQ无法提升模型固有的量化友好性，而QAT的全参数联合更新导致骨干权重与校准参数之间存在梯度耦合，两者均存在优化瓶颈。

Method: 提出Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP)：1）识别BN仿射参数为低维度高杠杆子空间，支配量化鲁棒性；2）PTQ模式下冻结骨干权重，仅在目标伪量化图下微调仿射参数，主动提升量化友好性；3）QAT模式下采用交替QAT-NAP训练范式，解耦特征学习与数值校准；4）通过蒸馏引导NAP实现定向平坦度优化，将师生logit差异投影到受限子空间。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上验证：NAP能恢复严重坍缩的低比特量化性能，持续提升基于重构的PTQ，并以极低的调优成本超越饱和的全参数QAT。理论分析证明BN仿射参数能够完全抵消量化失真的逐通道仿射分量，非线性舍入与裁剪残差构成不可约误差边界。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了低维定向子空间优化的原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角，表明通过针对归一化仿射参数这一关键子空间进行优化，可以在极小代价下显著提升低比特量化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [118] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了GeoMAR框架，通过双输入特征提取管道获取基于组件的地理描述，并利用KV-Q交换策略注入低质量特征，同时将单步映射重构为多步掩码自回归过程，在合成和真实基准上取得具有竞争力的盲脸修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于码本的盲脸修复方法在严重退化条件下常面临模糊的条件特征和脆弱的预测机制问题，影响修复质量。

Method: 采用双输入提取管道获取基于组件的几何描述特征，通过对齐几何先验注入器（使用KV-Q交换策略）整合文本先验与低质量特征，生成几何对齐特征；并将单步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）过程，以粗到细的方式逐步细化复杂面部区域。

Result: 在一个合成和三个真实世界基准数据集上，GeoMAR相比现有方法取得了高竞争力的感知质量和连贯的视觉结构。

Conclusion: GeoMAR通过几何对齐特征与掩码自回归细化相结合，有效解决了基于码本盲脸修复中条件特征模糊和预测机制脆弱的问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [119] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的指纹分离方法，将重叠指纹分离问题建模为图像修复任务，通过多阶段渐进式学习（从预训练Stable Diffusion出发，逐步引入指纹先验、部分指纹补全和重叠感知修复），在两个公开数据集上实现了高概率的匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊纹在犯罪现场潜在指纹和活体扫描场景中频繁出现，现有方法要么依赖需要强领域知识的基于规则的脊纹方向场补全，要么训练不考虑领域特定因素的端到端深度神经网络，缺乏有效且鲁棒的分离方案。

Method: 将指纹分离问题重构为图像修复（inpainting）任务，从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发，采用多阶段渐进式训练：1)整合指纹先验；2)增加部分指纹补全能力；3)提出基于多通道条件的重叠感知扩散修复模型，分别重建每个分量指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，使用所提出的基于扩散的修复方法重建的分量指纹，能够以非常高的概率与其匹配对应指纹成功匹配。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的渐进式多阶段训练策略，结合重叠感知的多通道条件修复机制，能够有效解决重叠指纹分离问题，并在指纹匹配精度上取得显著效果。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [120] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-Design是一个以语义RGBA图层为原子单元的图像生成框架，包含T2RGBA（文本生成独立RGBA资产）和I2L（图像分解为完整语义RGBA图层）两个模型，支持指令驱动的图层分解与编辑。在Crello基准上，I2L的逐层RGB L1误差降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU相对提升34%；T2RGBA的CLIP Score优于LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成基于像素合成，但人类设计师通过图层来创建、理解和编辑视觉内容。像素只定义图像的渲染方式，而图层定义图像的创建、理解和编辑方式，因此需要为多模态生成模型引入图层原生的设计空间。

Method: 提出UniWorld-Design框架，包含两个模型：（1）Text-to-RGBA（T2RGBA）模型直接从文本生成独立的RGBA资产；（2）Image-to-Layer（I2L）模型以完整图像、全局指令和逐层提示为条件，联合生成有序且完整的语义RGBA图层，支持顶层分解、递归分解和目标提取等指令接口。

Result: 在Crello基准上，I2L模型将逐层RGB L1误差降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU相对提升34%（对比Qwen-Image-Layered）；T2RGBA模型在CLIP Score上达到最高，超越LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: 通过将图像生成从像素合成重构为基于语义图层的结构化视觉组合，UniWorld-Design使图层成为可指令寻址的编辑操作单元，实现了更高质量、更强可编辑性的图像生成。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [121] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit是一个160亿参数的回归式扩散框架，实现无需未来帧和预定时长的实时视频编辑，通过块级自回归适配、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏和长程自回归蒸馏，在单张Nvidia B200 GPU上以约30 FPS实现720p视频编辑，性能优于现有流式编辑系统并与离线系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟因果生成和受限计算资源，同时保持源保真度和长期时间一致性，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 采用块级自回归适配（chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation）、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）和长程自回归蒸馏（Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation）三种技术，分别解决训练-推理不匹配、两步生成中的源保真度以及累积时间漂移问题。

Result: 自动和人工评估显示，JoyAI-Video-Edit在短视频和长视频上均大幅优于现有流式编辑系统，并与强离线系统保持竞争力；完整系统在单张Nvidia B200 GPU上实现约30 FPS的端到端720p视频编辑。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过自回归扩散与蒸馏策略结合，可以在不牺牲质量和保真度的前提下实现高性能的实时视频编辑。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [122] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch（Video-DR），将多模态智能体从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决了现有模型中的模态偏差和参数知识泄漏问题，通过解耦的感知-探索流水线和阶段式工具解锁机制，配合两阶段训练策略，在Video-DR-Bench基准上以35B-A3B模型达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）、GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体在处理视频任务时存在两个关键瓶颈：（1）模态偏差——智能体倾向于使用文本搜索而非视觉工具；（2）参数知识泄漏——模型依赖内部记忆而非真正的工具增强执行。这两大问题严重限制了模型在密集时空定位与开放网络探索相结合的视频场景中的表现。

Method: 提出Video-DR框架，包含：（1）解耦的感知-探索流水线，通过阶段式工具解锁强制模型在网页检索前进行彻底的跨帧视觉定位；（2）两阶段训练方案，即监督微调（SFT）后接组相对策略优化（GRPO），实现自主探索并突破模仿学习的上限；（3）构建Video-DR-Bench基准，包含200个复杂的多跳VQA实例，采用人机协作方式构建。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）；30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet相当，证明了训练范式在紧凑规模下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过解耦感知-探索流水线和两阶段训练策略，有效解决了多模态智能体在视频场景中的模态偏差和知识泄漏问题，在紧凑模型规模下即可达到或超越顶级闭源模型性能，验证了所提训练范式的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [123] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC)，一种用于免训练开放词汇语义分割的即插即用模块，通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入来缩小语义鸿沟，在八个基准上显著提升六种代表性方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练开放词汇语义分割方法仅改进视觉表示，而将编码通用类别概念的文本嵌入视为固定分类参考，导致通用概念与具体实例视觉表示之间存在语义鸿沟，产生不完整掩码和错误预测。

Method: PTC包含两个阶段：(1) 感知阶段（Perceiving stage）：基于初始匹配分数选取可靠的视觉证据构建类别特定视觉原型；(2) 锚定阶段（Anchoring stage）：利用视觉原型校准对应的文本嵌入，并根据视觉证据数量自适应调整校准强度。该方法无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为即插即用模块。

Result: 在八个基准数据集的广泛实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性分割方法的性能，生成更完整、更准确的分割结果。

Conclusion: PTC通过视觉-文本对齐的改进，验证了其作为一种简单有效的增强免训练开放词汇语义分割方法性能的可行性。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [TabletCraft: Bridging a 4,000-Year Cultural Gap with Bidirectional Akkadian NMT and Cuneiform Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02609)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabletCraft，这是首个支持美索不达米亚楔形文字双向交互的开源系统，允许用户阅读古代泥板（阿卡德语到英语）并创作新的楔形文字泥板（英语到阿卡德语到楔形文字到渲染泥板），填补了此前仅支持单向翻译的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存约50万块楔形文字泥板，但现代用户无法读写世界上最古老的文字系统，造成4000年的文化鸿沟。现有NLP工具仅支持学者向的阿克卡德语到英语单向翻译，非专业用户无法用楔形文字创作新内容，只能被动消费古代文化而非主动参与。

Method: TabletCraft系统包含三个核心组件：基于ByT5的翻译模型（在116K双向样本上训练）、包含14,240个映射的楔形文字符号转换器（覆盖率95.3%）、以及可视化的泥板渲染器。系统打包为pip可安装工具包，提供命令行界面和Web演示。

Result: 在Akkademia验证集（2,812个样本）上，阿克卡德语到英语的BLEU分数为49.1，英语到阿克卡德语的BLEU分数为48.5，这是反向翻译方向的首次公开定量结果。

Conclusion: TabletCraft是首个能够实现楔形文字双向交互的开源系统，在翻译质量上取得了良好成绩，特别是首次发布了英语到阿克卡德语方向的定量评估结果，使非专业用户也能主动参与美索不达米亚文化的创作与交流。

Abstract: Half a million cuneiform clay tablets survive in museums worldwide, yet modern users can neither read nor write in the world's oldest writing system, leaving a 4,000-year cultural barrier that existing NLP tools have only partially addressed.
  Prior work enables one-way, scholar-oriented translation from Akkadian to English, but offers no path in the reverse direction: non-specialist users cannot compose new content in cuneiform, and therefore remain passive consumers of ancient culture rather than active participants.
  We present TabletCraft, the first open-source system that enables bidirectional interaction with Mesopotamian writing. Users can read ancient tablets (Akkadian to English) and compose new messages as cuneiform clay tablets (English to Akkadian to cuneiform to rendered tablet).
  The system integrates a ByT5-based translation model trained on 116K bidirectional samples, a cuneiform sign converter with 14,240 mappings (95.3% coverage), and a visual tablet renderer, packaged as a pip-installable toolkit with CLI and web demo.
  On the held-out Akkademia validation split (2,812 samples), we report 49.1 BLEU for Akkadian-to-English and 48.5 BLEU for English-to-Akkadian, the first published quantitative result in the reverse direction.

</details>


### [125] [BBOWP-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Black-Box Optimization Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02612)
*Yutaro Yamada,Kei Hiroshima,Nozomu Yoshinari,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出黑箱优化文字问题（BBOWP）这一新问题设定，要求系统从自然语言描述中推断搜索空间和优化算法，并构建了BBOWP-Bench基准数据集用于评估LLM在该任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题的表述质量直接影响最终解的质量，但从自然语言自动推导优化问题是一个重要挑战。现有基准仅关注目标和约束可显式写成数学表达式的情形，而许多实际问题本质上是黑箱优化（BBO），其函数形式不可得，搜索空间设计和算法选择至关重要。目前缺乏自动化这些过程的基准和评估。

Method: 论文引入BBOWP问题设定，要求系统从自然语言描述中同时推断搜索空间和优化算法；建立BBOWP-Bench基准套件，每个实例包含自然语言问题描述、可执行评估环境和人工设计的基线公式，可同时评估搜索空间设计和算法选择。

Result: 对LLM的首个评估显示，当前LLM能够根据给定的评估预算选择合适的优化算法，但在搜索空间设计上存在不足，特别是在问题描述信息较少或搜索空间高度问题特定时，难以识别重要变量并平衡其范围。

Conclusion: BBOWP-Bench为黑箱优化的自动化问题表述提供了首个基准，揭示了LLM在算法选择上的潜力以及在搜索空间设计上的局限，为该方向的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Formulating an optimization problem strongly affects the quality of the final solution, yet good formulations usually require substantial expertise. Recent studies have therefore examined how to automatically derive optimization problems from natural-language descriptions, but existing benchmarks focus on settings where objectives and constraints can be written explicitly as mathematical expressions. Many practically important problems are naturally treated as black-box optimization (BBO) problems, in which only objective values are observable, and the functional form is unavailable. In BBO, the search space design, a part of the problem formulation, and the selection of the optimization algorithm are crucial for problem-solving. Automating these processes with large language models (LLMs) is a significant challenge. This paper introduces Black-Box Optimization Word Problems (BBOWP), a novel problem setting in which a system must infer both a search space and an optimization algorithm from a natural-language description of a black-box optimization task. To support research on this setting, we establish the BBOWP Benchmark Suite (BBOWP-Bench), a dataset and evaluation framework for BBOWP. Each instance combines a natural-language problem description, an executable evaluation environment, and a human-designed baseline formulation, allowing evaluation of both search-space design and algorithm selection. Using this benchmark, we provide the first evaluation of LLMs and show that current LLMs are capable of selecting suitable algorithms based on the given evaluation budget. However, they sometimes struggle with search space design, particularly in identifying important variables and balancing their ranges when the problem description is less informative or the search space is highly problem-specific. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/shiralab/bbowp-bench.

</details>


### [126] [MemArena: An Ego-Centric Benchmark for On-Device Agentic Personal Memory Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02613)
*Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemArena是一个面向边缘端个人记忆助手的对话基准测试，基于MASim模拟器生成50个代理在15天内的单一世界对话数据（1030万token），并评测六种记忆相关的推理与可信度维度。实验发现记忆后端的选择对内容准确性的影响大于阅读器模型的规模，权限感知的访问机制在所有后端上都表现不佳，且搜索延迟仅在极小模型上构成瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆基准测试未能充分覆盖活动密集型交互、自我中心视角和连贯的多会话世界这三者的组合场景，而这些正是边缘端个人记忆助手需要处理的关键场景。

Method: 构建MASim代理模拟器生成单一世界的对话基准MemArena：50个代理、15天活动期、1030万token的对话文本，每日每代理2.41万token的自我中心观察。与交互历史共同生成六种评估维度的ground truth（回忆、推理、可信度）。评估五种开源阅读器配合四种记忆后端（Vanilla context、BM25-RAG、Oracle retrieval、Memobase、MemSearch）。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1)记忆后端的选择比阅读器规模更影响内容准确性，在Qwen3-0.6B上Memobase到MemSearch提升+32.5/+19.2pp，超过阅读器扩展带来的+10.6/+6.8pp；(2)权限感知访问机制在所有后端上均失败，Oracle严重泄露而其他后端过于保守不敢披露；(3)搜索延迟仅在极小型阅读器上显著，在Spark GB10边缘节点上记忆搜索仅增加87/7/48ms（BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch）的固定延迟。

Conclusion: 记忆后端架构对边缘端个人记忆助手的重要性被低估，权限感知的隐私保护机制亟需更有效的设计，同时边缘端推理的延迟开销在当前架构下整体可控。代码、MASim模拟器和MemArena-L基准将在论文被接收后发布。

Abstract: Edge-deployed personal memory assistants must handle private interpersonal conversations on-device with open-weight models. Yet, existing memory benchmarks often under-test the combination of activity-dense interaction, ego-centric perspective, and coherent multi-session worlds. MemArena fills these gaps with a single-world conversational benchmark built with its MASim agent simulator, for 50 agents over 15 days (10.3M dialog-text tokens, 24.1K text-only ego-observed tokens/agent/day). With the interaction history, it co-generates ground truth over six recall, reasoning, and trustworthiness evaluation dimensions. We evaluate five open-weight readers with Vanilla context, BM25-RAG, Oracle retrieval, Memobase, and MemSearch as memory backends. Three results stand out: (1) Memory-backend choice matters more for content accuracy: At Qwen3-0.6B, Memobase-to-MemSearch gains +32.5/+19.2 pp, exceeding MemSearch reader scaling (+10.6/+6.8 pp). (2) Permission-aware access fails universally, with Oracle leaking heavily and other backends too timid to disclose. (3) Search latency bites only at very small reader: on a Spark GB10 edge node, memory-search adds a moderate and fixed 87/7/48 ms (BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch) that composes a small part of TTFT for most reader-backend combinations. Code, the MASim simulator, and the MemArena-L benchmark will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [127] [OncoTriad-QA: A Patient-Level Radiology-Pathology-Genomics Benchmark for Pan-Cancer Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02615)
*Ahnaf Munir,Dannong Wang,Michael W. McDonald,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了OncoTriad-QA，一个患者级别的泛癌问答基准，整合放射影像、病理、基因组学和临床元数据，包含86.1k个语义问题，覆盖9,281个TCGA患者案例和32种癌症队列。同时引入OncoVLM多模态模型，通过微调后在综合癌症问答任务上显著优于现有医学LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型和视觉-语言模型基准大多聚焦于单一模态或狭窄的图像-文本任务，缺乏对患者级别、跨多种证据流（放射学、病理学、基因组学、临床元数据）的肿瘤综合评估。

Method: 构建OncoTriad-QA基准，包含多模态数据配准（CT/MRI、全切片病理、体细胞突变、拷贝数变异、DNA甲基化、bulk RNA-seq和临床元数据），采用来源可溯源的LLM辅助标注流程生成案例特异性问题，并包含自动一致性检查和临床医师审阅。同时提出OncoVLM模型，通过学习投影器将模态原生数据映射到大语言模型接口。

Result: 现有通用和医学LLM在综合泛癌QA任务上表现有限，尤其在需要整合影像、形态学和分子证据的问题上。微调后的OncoVLM在MCQ准确率和BERTScore-F1上平均超过MedGemma-4B达10.7个百分点，在单选和开放式问题中均有一致提升。

Conclusion: OncoTriad-QA基准对训练和评估综合癌症问答模型具有重要价值，能够有效推动跨模态患者级别肿瘤评估能力的发展。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis and characterization require integrating complementary evidence from radiology, pathology, genomics, and clinical metadata. However, most medical large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks focus on isolated modalities or narrow image-text tasks, leaving patient-level oncology assessment across multiple evidence streams largely untested. We introduce OncoTriad-QA, a patient-level radiology-pathology-genomics benchmark for pan-cancer question answering. OncoTriad-QA contains 86.1k semantic questions across 9,281 TCGA patient cases from 32 cancer cohorts, aligning CT/MRI radiology, whole-slide histopathology, somatic mutations, copy-number alterations, DNA methylation, bulk RNA-seq, and clinical metadata. Case-specific annotations are constructed through a source-grounded LLM-assisted pipeline using curated labels, diagnostic reports, molecular profiles, and modality-derived evidence as primary sources of truth, with automated consistency checks and clinician review. We also introduce OncoVLM, a reference multimodal model that maps modality-native radiology, pathology, DNA methylation, and RNA-seq evidence into an LLM interface through learned projectors. Experiments show that existing general-purpose and medical LLMs remain limited on comprehensive pan-cancer QA, especially when questions require integrating imaging findings, tumor morphology, and molecular evidence. After fine-tuning on OncoTriad-QA, OncoVLM exceeds MedGemma-4B by an average of 10.7 points when using MCQ accuracy and BERTScore-F1, with consistent gains across multiple-choice and open-ended questions under radiology-only, pathology-only, and all-available settings. These results demonstrate the benchmark's value for training and evaluating models for integrated cancer question answering.

</details>


### [128] [Evaluating OpenAI's Privacy Filter: Cross-Lingual, Cross-Domain PII Detection Across 42 Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02616)
*Rohith Uppala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了OpenAI的隐私过滤器（OPF）——一个15亿参数的PII检测器，在42个合成基准（22种语言、5个领域）上的表现。OPF在结构化PII数据上优于Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa，但在叙事散文和非拉丁文字场景下性能急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛部署，个人身份信息（PII）保护变得至关重要。OpenAI的隐私过滤器作为商业化的PII检测工具，缺乏独立的系统性评估，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供公正的性能基准。

Method: 在42个合成基准测试上系统评估OPF，覆盖22种语言和5个领域（医疗、法律、金融、客户支持、结构化数据）。与Presidio、XLM-RoBERTa和GPT-4o进行对比，并进行错误分析和跨脚本、跨领域的性能分解。

Result: OPF零样本在AI4Privacy上F1=0.855、SPY医疗上F1=0.464，优于Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa；但在13种Indic和非拉丁语言上落后于XLM-RoBERTa。GPT-4o在医疗、法律、金融PII上领先。OPF在叙事散文场景下F1仅0.04-0.57，阿拉伯语0.04、西里尔文0.03，性能严重退化。结构性规则PII（email 0.78、phone 0.76）表现最好，文化多变的类型（person 0.40、address 0.49）最弱。

Conclusion: OPF在结构化、规范化PII检测上表现优异，但在自然语言叙述和多语言（尤其非拉丁文字）场景下存在明显短板。其精度跨度大（0.31-0.86），在高召回偏向下可能产生较多误报，部署时需根据具体场景审慎选择。

Abstract: We present the first independent, systematic evaluation of OpenAI's Privacy Filter (OPF), a 1.5B-parameter bidirectional PII detector, across 42 synthetic benchmarks spanning 22 languages and 5 domains. Zero-shot, OPF achieves F1=0.855 on AI4Privacy and 0.464 on SPY medical, outperforming Presidio (0.431, 0.273) and XLM-RoBERTa (0.269, 0.111) on PII-annotated benchmarks; on multilingual NER, XLM-RoBERTa leads OPF on all 13 Indic and non-Latin languages. GPT-4o leads on medical, legal, and financial PII (SPY: 0.643 avg, Gretel: 0.527), while OPF leads on structured synthetic PII (0.71 avg) and customer support (0.60). OPF degrades sharply when PII is embedded in narrative prose: F1=0.04--0.57 on NER benchmarks and collapse for non-Latin scripts (Arabic: 0.04, Cyrillic: 0.03). Error analysis shows OPF is strongest on structurally regular PII types (email: 0.78, phone: 0.76) and weakest on culturally variable ones (person: 0.40, address: 0.49), and is recall-biased on customer-support and medical/legal PII (P=0.31--0.54, R=0.70--0.85); global precision spans 0.31--0.86 across all domains.

</details>


### [129] [Preferred, Not Safer: Pairwise Preference Is a Poor Proxy for Clinical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02617)
*Fay Elhassan,David Sasu,Alexandra Kulinkina,Lars Henning Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用MOOVE平台上的26,804条临床医生成对偏好判断（覆盖13个LLM、736+名临床医生、28+国家）评估LLM临床安全性，发现临床医生偏好信号并不能可靠反映临床安全，表面特征对偏好的解释力甚至超过安全关键指标。作者提出临床调整偏好排名（结合成对偏好与量表反馈），可生成更安全敏感的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床环境中LLM的安全评估至关重要，但现有的成对偏好评估可能隐藏或掩盖安全关键性缺陷。作者希望验证临床医生成对偏好能否作为临床安全的可靠代理信号，并找出其中的盲区。

Method: 利用MOOVE平台收集临床医生对13个LLM输出的成对偏好判断和多元标准量表评分（-2到+2，负值表示不安全/误导）。通过统计分析偏好排名与安全评估指标的关联，分解偏好变化中安全关键特征与表面特征的贡献，并引入结合偏好与量表反馈的临床调整偏好排名。

Result: 临床医生成对偏好在安全关键表现上是较差的代理指标；偏好排名高的模型仍可能在Harmlessness和Accuracy维度有大量临床意义上的失败（≤-1）。失败在专科间分布不均，形成跨领域"禁行区"，在聚合排名和单分数排行榜中不可见。相当比例的偏好投票不含正向安全信号，表面特征对偏好变化的解释力略高于安全关键量表差异。

Conclusion: 在临床决策中排序LLM时，应将偏好评估与安全评估分离，直接报告安全关键失败率，并纳入临床调整排名机制，以获得更安全的LLM选择和部署。

Abstract: We evaluate whether clinician pairwise preferences provide a reliable signal of clinical safety in large language model (LLM) evaluation using expert feedback from MOOVE (Massive Open Online Validation and Evaluation), a clinician-led platform collecting blinded pairwise preferences alongside multi-criterion rubric ratings. Clinicians assign scores on a discrete $[-2, +2]$ scale, where negative values indicate clinically unsafe or misleading content. Using 26{,}804 pairwise judgments across outputs from 13 LLMs, contributed by more than 736 clinicians across 28+ countries, we find that clinician preference is a poor proxy for safety-critical performance. Models ranking highly under pairwise preference can still exhibit substantial rates of clinically meaningful failures ($\leq -1$) on dimensions such as \emph{Harmlessness} and \emph{Accuracy}. These failures are unevenly distributed across specialties, creating domain-specific ``no-go zones'' not visible in aggregate rankings or single-number leaderboards. We further analyze contributing factors including prompt length, refusal and escalation behavior, and the relative contributions of safety-critical versus surface-level features. A substantial fraction of preference votes carry no positive safety signal, while feature decomposition shows that surface-level characteristics explain slightly more preference variation than safety-critical rubric differences. Finally, we introduce a clinically adjusted preference ranking combining pairwise preference with rubric-derived feedback, producing a more safety-aware ordering than raw Bradley--Terry strength alone. Our findings support evaluation practices that separate preference from safety, report safety-critical failure rates directly, and incorporate clinically grounded adjustments when ranking LLMs for clinical decision making.

</details>


### [130] [JudgeArena: A Unified Framework for Reproducible LLM-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02620)
*Erlis Lushtaku,Bora Kargi,Ali Elganzory,Fabio Ferreira,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Julia Kreutzer,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JudgeArena是一个开源框架，统一了多个主流LLM裁判基准（AlpacaEval、Arena-Hard、MT-Bench和m-Arena-Hard），支持可替换的裁判模型、全面的元数据记录，并提供了可与闭源裁判模型媲美的开源裁判配置，还能通过结合人类标注与LLM裁判评估来高精度模拟LMArena Elo分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge评估生态碎片化严重：各基准附带独立代码库、硬编码特定闭源裁判模型、仅支持单一评估协议，导致难以系统研究基准、裁判模型、提示词和推理后端等设计选择对模型质量结论的影响。

Method: 构建统一开源框架JudgeArena，整合多个主流裁判基准到单一接口；支持通过vLLM、llama.cpp或OpenRouter访问的任何模型同时作为候选模型和裁判模型；调优开源模型的裁判配置以匹配或超越闭源裁判模型；结合人类标注与LLM裁判评估来模拟LMArena Elo分数。

Result: 调优后的开源裁判配置在英语和多语言人类偏好数据集上匹配或超越了闭源裁判模型；通过结合人类标注与LLM裁判评估，能高精度模拟LMArena Elo分数，提供开放且低成本的评估替代方案。

Conclusion: JudgeArena通过统一接口和可替换组件显著提升了LLM评估的透明度、可复现性和系统性，降低了闭源裁判模型的依赖，并为大规模人类标注提供了实用的低成本替代方案。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge evaluation has become a dominant paradigm for ranking language models, yet the ecosystem remains fragmented: most benchmarks ship their own code base, hardcode a specific closed-model judge, and support a single evaluation protocol. This fragmentation makes it difficult to study how design choices--the benchmark, the judge model, the prompt, the inference backend--affect the conclusions we draw about model quality. We introduce JudgeArena, an open-source framework that unifies major LLM-judge benchmarks (AlpacaEval, Arena-Hard, MT-Bench, and m-Arena-Hard) under a single interface with swappable judges and comprehensive metadata logging for increased transparency in reporting and reproducibility. It enables systematic studies of judge choices, as any model accessible via vLLM, llama.cpp, or OpenRouter can serve as both candidate and judge. Furthermore, JudgeArena ships with tuned judge configurations for open models that match or outperform closed-model judges, validated on human preference datasets in both English and multilingual settings, reducing the reliance on opaque closed models. Finally, by combining existing human annotations with LLM-judge evaluations of a target model, JudgeArena can simulate LMArena Elo scores with high accuracy offering a practical, open, and low-cost alternative to large-scale human annotation campaigns.

</details>


### [131] [Knowing the Form, Not the Function: Automatically Auditing Answer--Authority Decoupling in Legal Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02621)
*Hsien-Jyh Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文測試法律基準測試中"答案正確性"是否可以作為"法條援引正確性"的代理指標。通過對238道台灣律師考試題目的分析，發現LLMs的答案正確與法條援引在兩個方向上都存在分離：刑事法律中24.0-42.4%的答案是對的但漏掉正確法條，15.2-21.7%答案錯誤但援引了正確法條。因此提出應對法律基準測試進行"答案-法條"聯合評估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 現有的法律基準測試僅以最終答案評分，即使模型同時陳述了法律依據。作者想檢驗答案正確性能否代表法條援引的準確性，以評估是否存在系統性的分離現象。

Method: 使用4個LLM在238道台灣律師考試題目上進行測試，這些題目都有經過驗證的適用條文。使用自動化方法同時審查答案正確性和法條援引正確性。此外進行了法條檢索探針測試和允許不援引法條的干預實驗，並進行了初步的中國民事法律擴展測試。

Result: 在刑事法律中，24.0-42.4%的合法回應是答案正確但漏掉正確法條，15.2-21.7%是答案錯誤但援引了正確法條。法條檢索探針和干預實驗證實答案與援引行為可以在輸出層面獨立移動。

Conclusion: 傳統的答案-only評分會將自然發生的法條遺漏視為完整的基準測試成功，這是不合理的。由於法條在結構上可提取且外部可驗證，這一缺陷可以自動化衡量。因此建議法律基準測試應採用"答案-法條聯合評估"框架。

Abstract: Legal benchmarks typically score final answers even when models also state legal authority. We test whether answer correctness can serve as a proxy for authority grounding. Under ordinary reasoning prompts that did not request statutory citations, four LLMs spontaneously produced authority markers across 238 Taiwan bar-examination items. Because each item has a verified governing provision, we automatically audit answer correctness and authority grounding jointly. The two dimensions dissociate in both directions. In criminal law, 24.0--42.4\% of valid responses were answer-correct but missed the gold authority, while 15.2--21.7\% were answer-incorrect but cited it. A separate statutory-retrieval probe and a permissive citation-abstention intervention further show that answer and citation behavior can move separately at the output level. Because this mismatch arises without adversarial or inconsistency-inducing prompting, answer-only scoring treats naturally occurring gold-authority misses as complete benchmark successes. Because statutory authority is structurally extractable and externally verifiable, the failure can be measured automatically. A preliminary PRC civil-law extension also observes citation-unrequested authority marking, motivating a full cross-jurisdictional joint audit. We therefore propose joint answer--authority evaluation for statute-grounded legal benchmarks.

</details>


### [132] [Speculative Correction: Draft-then-Refine Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02625)
*Brian K Chen,Chong Wu,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的"先草稿后精炼"推理模式：先用模型生成完整草稿，再通过双向扩散进行全局精炼。在LLaDA2.1模型上验证了两种配置：同模型草稿-精炼（Flash-Flash）和异质模型投机校正（Mini-Flash），两者在质量和延迟之间取得了有利的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）具有双向token修正能力，但现有解码过程通常将其强制适配为从左到右的分块生成方式，未能充分发挥其双向精炼的潜力。作者希望探索一种简单的即插即用推理模式来释放DLM的全局精炼能力。

Method: 提出两种配置：(1) Flash-Flash：同一Flash模型先以块自回归方式生成草稿，再以扩散方式进行全局精炼；(2) Mini-Flash：借鉴投机解码思想，用Mini模型草拟完整回复，再用Flash模型作为可编辑初始化进行修正。通过因果消融实验（完全掩码初始化、全局vs局部精炼）来验证各组件贡献。

Result: Flash-Flash将GSM8K-384准确率从0.848提升至0.899（同时比基线快1.20倍），MBPP-384从0.545提升至0.693。Mini-Flash在MATH-384上达到0.294（Flash为0.300），但速度快2.17倍。延迟匹配对照组和因果消融验证了草稿初始化和全局精炼的贡献。

Conclusion: 同模型草稿-精炼模式证明双向精炼是DLM有用的解码原语；投机校正是无需训练即可实现快速DLM生成的路径。但异质模型级联并未在所有指标上匹配Flash质量，应解释为Pareto前沿权衡而非统一超越。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) can revise tokens bidirectionally, but standard decoding procedures often adapt them to left-to-right generation by producing text block by block. We study a simple plug-and-play inference pattern: first generate a complete draft, then refine the full response using bidirectional diffusion. Using LLaDA2.1-Flash and LLaDA2.1-Mini, we evaluate two configurations. In Flash-Flash, the same Flash model serves as both drafter and refiner, testing whether an existing model can improve its own block-autoregressive output through global refinement. In Mini-Flash, inspired by speculative decoding, we introduce speculative correction: Mini drafts a full response, and Flash revises it as an editable initialization. Flash-Flash improves GSM8K-384 accuracy from 0.848 to 0.899 while running 1.20 times faster than the selected Flash block-autoregressive baseline, and improves MBPP-384 from 0.545 to 0.693. Latency-window-matched Flash-only controls indicate that these gains persist after targeted tuning of block-autoregressive decoding. Causal ablations indicate that completed drafts provide useful initializations: refinement from a fully masked span performs poorly, full global refinement provides a clear additional gain on GSM8K, and local refinement captures much of the gain on MBPP and MATH. Mini-Flash provides useful quality-latency trade-offs, including MATH-384 performance of 0.294 versus 0.300 for Flash while running 2.17 times faster. These results support a Pareto-frontier interpretation rather than the claim that the heterogeneous cascade uniformly matches Flash quality. Overall, same-model draft-and-refine provides evidence that bidirectional refinement is a useful decoding primitive for DLMs, while speculative correction demonstrates a training-free route to fast DLM generation.

</details>


### [133] [Stuck on "A": Diagnosing and Repairing Interface Injury in Attention-to-KDA Linearization of a 0.6B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02689)
*Ronglong Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将Qwen3-0.6B-Base中28个全注意力层中的21层转换为KDA线性注意力层，在单张消费级GPU预算下发现模型虽在困惑度上接近教师模型，但多选题准确率仍接近随机水平（25-29% vs 教师50.6%）。通过四排列诊断发现模型依赖选项标签而非内容，这一"接口损伤"可通过1,000步格式定向KL蒸馏修复（+12.48分），且伴随FP32主权重失败模式的工程经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将全注意力层转换为线性注意力层后模型具体被破坏了什么，揭示标准蒸馏指标（如困惑度、KL散度）无法捕捉的性能缺陷，并找出修复方法。

Method: 将21/28个全注意力层转换为KDA线性注意力层，采用隐藏状态对齐和端到端KL蒸馏；设计四排列诊断（旋转选项标签）来分离标签依赖与内容理解；采用1,000步格式定向completion-only KL蒸馏、persona SFT和一轮on-policy DPO进行修复。

Result: 转换后困惑度接近教师但C-Eval准确率仅25-29%（接近随机），模型81%时间预测"A"且106/161题在四种排列下保持同一标签；格式定向KL阶段将准确率提升+12.48点并减半标签粘性；后续SFT和DPO在噪声范围内保持分数。

Conclusion: 线性注意力转换的主要损伤在于输出格式/接口层而非语义内容理解，标准蒸馏指标无法检测此缺陷，但针对性的格式训练可有效修复；同时公开了完整工程经验包括FP32主权重防止bf16优化器更新被吞没等关键教训。

Abstract: We convert 21 of 28 full-attention layers of Qwen3-0.6B-Base into KDA (Kimi Delta Attention) linear-attention layers on a single consumer-grade GPU budget, and ask a simple question: what exactly does the conversion break? After surgery, hidden-state alignment and end-to-end KL distillation drive the student close to its teacher in perplexity, yet multiple-choice accuracy stays near random chance (25-29% vs. the teacher's 50.6% on C-Eval). Using a four-permutation diagnostic that rotates answer options while holding content fixed, we show the model sticks to option labels (predicting "A" 81% of the time; 106/161 questions keep the same label under all four rotations) rather than following answer content -- an interface injury that standard distillation metrics cannot see. A 1,000-step format-targeted completion-only KL stage repairs the interface (+12.48 points on C-Eval, label-stickiness roughly halved), after which persona SFT and one round of on-policy DPO preserve benchmark scores within noise. We release code, weights, recipes, and the full audit trail, and distill the engineering lessons -- including an FP32-master failure mode in which bf16 optimizer updates are silently swallowed -- that made convergence possible at this budget.

</details>


### [134] [Crayotter: Learning Long-Horizon Video Editing Agents via Group-Relative Preference Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02694)
*Lecheng Yan,Jianze Lin,Yichong Zhang,Ben Pan,Wenxi Li,Chenyang Lyu,Liting Zhou,Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.CL
